# MS.990 Parallel Edition: Canvases 365–394

## Page 403 — IIIF Canvas #365

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0365.JP2` (5827 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0365.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0365.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Contains recipes for head pills (including Pilula de Succino Cratonis and Roman pills), strong purging pills, pills to draw out watery phlegm, pills for trembling hands, gout/podagra pills, and pills to stop liver flux.

## Page 402 — IIIF Canvas #366

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0366.JP2` (5997 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0366.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0366.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,300

Presents pill recipes for lientery/white dysentery (marked with a pointing hand), chest and lung diseases, toothache, obstructions (Pilulae de opilata), dropsy, and five distinct recipes for head and eye pills.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Illustration on canvas 366** (`#xywh=900,500,4697,1400`): Small ink drawing of a pointing hand (manicule) with a cuff in the top-left margin beside the page number 402, pointing right toward the rubricated heading 'Pillule für Lienteria oder Weisse rüehr.'.

## Page 403 — IIIF Canvas #367

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0367.JP2` (5790 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0367.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0367.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Folio 403r contains twelve pharmaceutical pill recipes in Early New High German and Latin, targeting quartan fever, uterine prolapse, constipation, kidney stones, sciatica, headaches, and sharp humors. The page is arranged in two columns with rubricated red-ink headings and frequent apothecary and alchemical symbols.

## Page 404 — IIIF Canvas #368

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0368.JP2` (5998 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0368.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0368.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,550

Folio 404v introduces a new section on 'noble powders' (Edlen Puluer), headed by a watercolor illustration of four red cylindrical apothecary jars on a shelf. It records six recipes for cordial, cooling, and warming gemstone, pearl, and epilepsy powders in Early New High German and Latin.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Four apothecary jars on a shelf** (`#xywh=900,500,4698,1400`): Watercolor illustration at the top of the left column depicting four red cylindrical apothecary vessels/jars with rims standing in a row on a brown shelf against a plain background.

## Page 405 — IIIF Canvas #369

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0369.JP2` (5838 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0369.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0369.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,450

Folio 405r continues the collection of medicinal powders, including an arcanum for pregnant women against epilepsy, a head-bag powder, a carminative stomach powder, a heart-strengthening powder, two epilepsy powders, and astrological instructions for digging peony root. Rubricated red-ink headings and alchemical/astrological symbols appear throughout.

## Page 406 — IIIF Canvas #370

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0370.JP2` (6030 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0370.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0370.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Page 406v presents medicinal treatises on the virtues of dissolved pearls (compared to aurum potabile), a disclosure exposing counterfeit stag's heart-bones (Hirschkreuzel) substituted with ox-heart bones, and recipes for hartshorn jelly (Hirschsulze) and Magisterium of hartshorn, accompanied by a hand-colored illustration of a leaping stag.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Leaping stag** (`#xywh=900,500,4730,1400`): Hand-colored ink and watercolor illustration of a brown stag with antlers leaping to the left across green grassy terrain, framed below by a red ink horizontal line.

Page 407r concludes the Magisterium of hartshorn and presents an illustrated alchemical treatise on distilling philosophical hartshorn with cooling and cordial herbs (chicory, endive, sorrel, scorzonera, blessed thistle, lemon balm, borage) over five days to produce a restorative jelly for hot fevers.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Distillation apparatus and furnace** (`#xywh=400,500,4493,1400`): Hand-colored ink and watercolor illustration of an alchemical distillation setup: a brick furnace on the right topped with a copper alembic whose spout leads to a large brown receiver vessel resting on a wooden stand on the left, with a small black pot underneath and a building with a smoking chimney in the background.

## Page 410 — IIIF Canvas #374

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0374.JP2` (5972 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0374.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0374.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,650 / 2,350

Page 410 (verso) contains alchemical and workshop recipes in two columns, covering the preparation of gum pastilles (Gumj.), boiled oil (Faum öll.), pitch (Pöch), iron slag pigment (Scoria feri), calcined ivory (Helffen bain), a clarifying preparation (Heider Plätt zu L:), and a rubricated treatise on artificial gemstones (Edl stain Allerleÿ zu L:).

Page 411 (recto) continues an alchemical procedure for calcining, reverberating, and extracting gemstones with spiritus and vinegar, followed by a rubricated section on the medicinal virtues of oil of gemstones. The right column concludes a reference to Hieronymus Bock's Herbarium and presents two rubricated sections detailing the virtues of dissolved pearls and the method for dissolving pearls, corals, and crabs' eyes using lemon juice or spiritus and salt spirit.

## Page 412 — IIIF Canvas #376

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0376.JP2` (5997 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0376.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0376.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Page 412 presents a colored illustration of a distillation furnace and alembic followed by recipes for distilling herbal and floral spirits (scurvy-grass, elderberry, rose, wormwood, lily of the valley), a uterine spirit (Muetter Geist), and the beginning of a saffron elixir.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Distillation apparatus** (`#xywh=0,0,5997,6853`): Colored drawing of a brick distillation furnace with an open firebox showing red flames, surmounted by a metallic or grey alembic with a long descending spout leading into a spherical glass receiver resting on a low four-legged stand.

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |

## Page 415 — IIIF Canvas #379

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0379.JP2` (5893 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0379.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0379.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,450

Folio 415r presents a botanical illustration of Bryony root (Prijonia Wurzl) accompanied by a rubricated treatise on its purgative virtues against dropsy, chest phlegm, and uterine suffocation. The right column provides three compound extract recipes with apothecary weights, dosage instructions, and a rubricated NB warning on the milder potency of Bryony fecula.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Bryonia (Prijonia) root** (`#xywh=0,0,5893,6853`): Hand-colored botanical watercolor and ink illustration at the top of the left column depicting a large yellow-ochre tuberous taproot with three green leafy stems.

## Page 416 — IIIF Canvas #380

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0380.JP2` (6005 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0380.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0380.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,700

Folio 416v concludes the Bryony remedies with lozenge and uterine potion recipes, followed by a rubricated section on testing the purity of a chemical salt or nitre (marked with a circled 1) by burning it on a hot iron plate. The remainder of the page details the therapeutic uses of this preparation against malignant fevers, plague, kidney stones, liver obstruction, and throat inflammation.

An unnumbered recto folio featuring a full-page hand-colored illustration of an apothecary's workshop, showing a bearded monk in a brown habit holding a red flask behind a table laden with a mortar and pestle, vials, and boxes beneath four shelves of apothecary jars. The lower-right portion of the illustration is covered by a large blank paper repair patch, with modern pencil foliation 188 in the bottom-left corner.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Apothecary's workshop with monk** (`#xywh=0,0,5822,6853`): Full-page watercolor and ink illustration depicting four shelves of green, yellow, and terracotta apothecary jars above a leaded window, a yellow wall cabinet with black hinges, and a bearded monk in a brown habit holding a small red flask behind a red-draped table with a brass mortar and pestle, glass vials, and boxes; a distillation vessel sits at far left, and a large blank paper patch covers the lower-right half.

## Page 418 — IIIF Canvas #382

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0382.JP2` (6045 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0382.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0382.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 1,950

Verso folio (p. 418) opening a section on medicinal and alchemical oils, including a rubricated preface, a recipe for citron and bitter orange oil (partially obscured by a blank paper patch in the left column) illustrated with a watercolor distillation flask in the right column, followed by recipes for Oleum septem florum and Oleum anodynum (bryony oil).

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Distillation vessel (gläßl) for separating essential oils from water** (`#xywh=0,0,6045,6853`): Watercolor and ink illustration of a round blue-gray glass flask/receiver resting on a brownish-green mound with small yellow plant tufts, showing a narrow neck with a small attached side-receptacle or stopper at the top.

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0385.JP2` (5820 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0385.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0385.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,450

Page 421 contains Early New High German apothecary recipes for purgative extracts and electuaries, including a bryony extract, a gentle purgative electuary safe for pregnant women and children, a polypody-senna electuary, a complex turpeth-colocynth electuary against syphilis and phlegm, and an iron-based electuary for spleen and liver obstructions.

## Page 423 — IIIF Canvas #387

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0387.JP2` (5804 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0387.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0387.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,550

Page 423 features remedies for asthma, phthisis, tertian fever (including pulse plasters), a laxative cataplasm, and two procedures for purifying and distilling turpentine, accompanied by a colored drawing of a wooden filtration vessel.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Wie Man den Terpenthin Leütern sol (Turpentine Purification Vessel)** (`#xywh=0,0,5804,6853`): Colored ink and wash illustration in the upper-middle of the right column depicting a golden-yellow cylindrical linden-wood container (Pixen) resting atop a grey-blue cylindrical receiving vessel/glass on a brown wooden block, illustrating the filtration of turpentine.

## Page 425 — IIIF Canvas #389

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0389.JP2` (5803 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0389.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0389.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Contains recipes for floral and herbal tinctures, honeys, juleps, and juices (rose, violet, elderberry, mulberry, raspberry, scurvy-grass), followed by medicinal virtues of the common elder tree and elder syrup, illustrated with a rose blossom and an elder tree.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Rose blossom (Rosen Tinctur / Rosen Hönig)** (`#xywh=0,0,5803,6853`): Small hand-colored botanical drawing in the left column depicting a pink/red rose blossom with green leaves and a brown stem.

**Elder tree (Von Blusen Khraft vnd Dugent des gemainen hollers)** (`#xywh=0,0,5803,6853`): Medium hand-colored botanical drawing in the right column depicting a leafy green elder tree with a brown trunk standing on layered brown-ochre ground.

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0390.JP2` (6024 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0390.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0390.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Continues the elder syrup recipe and gives preparations of elder water and elder bark emetics/purgatives; the right column presents recipes for hair removal, a water-igniting stone, tartar emetic, balsam of sulfur, a melancholy sachet, and good ink, illustrated with a nettle sprig and an inkstand.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Nettle sprig (Das khain har Wachse)** (`#xywh=0,0,6024,6853`): Small hand-colored botanical drawing in the right column depicting a leafy sprig (likely Urtica/nettle) with yellowish-green leaves.

**Inkstand with quill pen (Guette Dintten Machen)** (`#xywh=0,0,6024,6853`): Small hand-colored drawing in the lower right column showing a cylindrical red-brown pot and a dark inkwell with a quill pen resting on a wooden desk.

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0391.JP2` (5734 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0391.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0391.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,450

## Page 429 — IIIF Canvas #393

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0393.JP2` (5858 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0393.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0393.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,550

Folio 429r concludes the fragrant cap-powder recipe and presents remedies for cress mucilage, heart cordials, vinegar making, an illustrated arum-root preparation (Jerja Serpentaria), ant oil for sciatica, and lozenges for melancholy.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Arum maculatum (Lords-and-Ladies / Cuckoo-pint)** (`#xywh=0,0,5858,6853`): Hand-colored botanical illustration in the upper-left margin of the right column showing three green leaves, a drooping bell-like flower in blue and yellow, and fibrous roots below.

**Red-ink heart ornaments** (`#xywh=0,0,5858,6853`): Two small solid red-ink heart symbols centered beneath the rubricated headings 'In Hizigen frenen beÿ dem' and 'Ain Herliche Hertz Stärckung.' in the left column.

**Red-ink wheel emblem** (`#xywh=0,0,5858,6853`): A small circular eight-spoked wheel or rosette drawn in red ink beneath the rubricated heading 'Zeltel für die Melancholischen Wind' in the right column.

## Page 430 — IIIF Canvas #394

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0394.JP2` (6029 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0394.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0394.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,250

Page 430 (verso) contains alchemical and medical recipes, including a gold solution for uterine ailments, depilatory and hair-retention ointments, cements for repairing broken glass and majolica vessels, a tincture of sal tartari for stomach and bile, a cephalic snuff powder, and a protective charm/ointment against gunfire.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Bust portrait of a young man in profile** (`#xywh=0,0,6029,6853`): Framed watercolor and pen-and-ink illustration in the left column beside the hair-loss recipes, showing a young man in profile facing left with wavy light-brown hair, wearing a red tunic with a wide yellow-ochre collar.

| 12 | leimb. hernach mach das glaß d[ar] die drümer | glue. Afterwards make the glass and the shards |
| 13 | ein weinig warm. ud füge die drüme[r] | a little warm, and fit the shards |
| 14 | zusame[n], laß druckhen werde[n], so helt es | together, let it become dry, so it holds |
| 15 | wögenolt. ud ist nicht der leim zu | firmly, and the glue is not to be |
| 16 | sehen. allein der bruch des glaß. | seen, only the break of the glass. |
| 17 | Tinctur. Tartari salis. für den Magen | Tincture of salt of tartar for the stomach *— Note: Rubricated heading in red ink* |
| 18 | und Gall. | and bile. *— Note: Rubricated heading in red ink* |
| 19 | ℞. ☉ Virid. ◻ Crud. āā. q. s. f. ♂ uiuum | Take: green gold/vitriol, crude tartar/alum, of each as much as suffices, living iron *— Note: Contains alchemical symbols ℞, ☉, ◻, āā, ♂* |
| 20 | alb dan calcinirs es in feür, ud loßs ud | then calcine it in fire, and dissolve it and |
| 21 | zeüchs durch ein Tuch. hernach laß abrauche[n] | draw it through a cloth. Afterwards let it evaporate |
| 22 | biß auf die drükhne. alb dan puluerisirs | until dryness. Then pulverize the |
| 23 | masa mit recti: ▽ q. s. laß es 8 tag stehe[n] | mass with rectified spirit/water as much as suffices, let it stand 8 days, *— Note: Contains symbol ▽ (water)* |
| 24 | hernach filtrirt. so hastu die tinctur salis. | afterwards filter it; thus you have the tincture of salt. |
| 25 | pro dos: 10 oder 12 tropffen. | For a dose: 10 or 12 drops. |
| 26 | Fluß Puluer in flüsigen haubt. | Catarrh powder in a rheumy head. *— Note: Rubricated heading in red ink* |
| 27 | ℞. brotblüet ℥i. haselwurz. ℥i. Zitwer | Take: ... blossoms 1 ounce, asarabacca 1 ounce, zedoary *— Note: Contains symbols ℞ and ℥i* |
| 28 | āā. ℥ij. rabarbar. Cinnam. Muscat nuß. Macis | of each 2 ounces, rhubarb, cinnamon, nutmeg, mace, *— Note: Contains symbols āā and ℥ij* |
| 29 | Galblüet. āā. ℥j. gariophil. galgant. ud | galanga blossoms, of each 1 ounce, cloves, galangal, and *— Note: Contains symbols āā and ℥j* |
| 30 | Sachari ℥iij. ℥ß. f. P. subtilissim[um] gib ein | sugar 3 1/2 ounces; make a very fine powder; give *— Note: Contains symbols ℥iij and ℥ß* |
| 31 | ein trünk nach dn mittag mal ℥i. | a draught after the midday meal, 1 ounce. *— Note: Contains symbol ℥i* |
| 32 | Das dich khein Feind khan schiesen | That no enemy can shoot you *— Note: Rubricated heading in red ink* |
| 33 | Weder Zu wunden stiche[n] noch zu[n]d[en] | neither to wound, stab, nor ignite *— Note: Rubricated heading in red ink* |
| 34 | ℞. Nimb ein od[er] 2 handtuoll Mellisen ud reibe | Take: take one or 2 handfuls of lemon balm and rub *— Note: Contains symbol ℞* |
| 35 | dich damit wo du bloss bist. in gesicht ud | yourself therewith where you are bare, on face and |
| 36 | hende. so versichere ich dich das du dar auf | hands; then I assure you that thereupon you |
| 37 | ganz unversehrt khanst hin geh[n] | can walk away completely unharmed, |
| 38 | so wird die khugl stärkhen ud khanst d[ar] auf | so the bullet will bounce off and you can thereupon |
| 39 | flochs faden anrührt. | touch it like a flax thread. |
